# Campaign-2 signature diff report — 2026-09-04

**Superseded by `docs/reports/2026-09-05-campaign2-rediff.md`.** deb's
`~/hbc2js` — the checkout the campaign2 runners actually execute from —
never received the P-16 (`b5362b0`) or fix-wave-3 (`5509ee5`) harness fixes
(confirmed: neither commit exists in that checkout's history), so every
number and signature below, including the "6,118 new signatures" headline,
is a **pre-fix** classification. The 2026-09-05 report re-diffs a sample
under the current (fixed) harness and finds most of the old top-10 example
seeds now PASS.

Generated by `tools/fuzz/diff-signatures.mjs` run on the `deb` box against all
8 `campaign2-*` directories under `~/hbc2js-fuzz` (checkout used for the run:
a throwaway `~/hbc2js-sigs` clone; `deb`'s `origin` for `~/hbc2js` was behind
the commit that introduced this tool, so `diff-signatures.mjs` +
`known-signatures.json` were `scp`'d over directly per the brief's fallback).
`~/hbc2js` itself was never touched — the 8 campaign-runner processes were
left running there throughout. Reports were already present under each
campaign's `reports/` dir; no finds needed re-running.

Campaigns were **still running live** while this report was generated (all 8
`campaign-runner.sh` processes were up the whole time — see "Runner status"
below), so the two aggregation passes below (the tool's own report, and a
companion frequency tally) were taken a few seconds apart and drift by a
handful of signatures; both are snapshots as of ~13:25 UTC 2026-09-04, not a
final count.

## New vs known signatures — summary

- **Distinct divergence signatures observed this run: 6,135** (`6,118` NEW +
  `17` KNOWN-still-firing, per `diff-signatures.mjs`'s own report below;
  keys capped to 300 chars per the tool's own truncation rule).
- **New (not in `tools/fuzz/known-signatures.json`'s 64 surviving signatures
  from `docs/reports/2026-09-04-finds-retriage-postfix.md`): 6,118 of
  6,135 (99.7%).** The overwhelming majority of divergence signatures in
  this campaign are new, i.e. not among the 64 previously-retriaged
  survivors — expected, since campaign-2 covers different construct-fuzz
  grammar/seed ranges (`2000000`, `3000000`) and adds v84 to the version
  matrix.
- **Known signatures still firing: 17 of 64** (see "## KNOWN signatures
  still firing" section below) — these are already-triaged bugs still
  reproducing in campaign-2, on v94/v96/v99 (v84 fires none of the 64).
- Caveat inherited from the tool itself (see its file header): signature
  keys are per-report-chunk deduplicated but **not** linked to a specific
  seed/find by the schema; "example" attributions below are heuristic
  (lexicographically-first find file, among campaigns containing the
  signature, whose version matches one of the signature's attributed
  versions) and are NOT verified repros — re-minimise before triage.

### Top 10 NEW signatures by report-occurrence count

"Report-occurrence count" = number of `reports/*.json` chunk files across
all 8 campaign dirs whose `signatures[]` array contains this (capped) key.
Each chunk covers 500 programs and the driver already de-dupes signatures
within a chunk, so this is a frequency proxy, not a find count — the schema
does not link signatures to individual finds/seeds (same caveat as above).
Computed with a one-off companion script (`/tmp/sigcount.mjs` on deb, not
committed — a trivial pre-dedup variant of the aggregation
`diff-signatures.mjs` already does).

## Runner status (deb, ~13:25 UTC 2026-09-04)

All 8 `campaign2-*` runners were alive (`pgrep -af campaign-runner.sh`
matched all 8 PIDs) throughout data collection for this report.

| campaign | pid | seeds tried (approx) | seeds/hour | finds so far | finds/hour | last log line |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| campaign2-v84-2000000 | 458290 | 2,500 | ~2,166 | 115 | ~99.6 | `2026-09-04T13:11:25Z v84 chunk 4 seed-base=2002000 count=500` |
| campaign2-v84-3000000 | 458291 | 2,500 | ~2,178 | 44 | ~38.3 | `2026-09-04T13:11:02Z v84 chunk 4 seed-base=3002000 count=500` |
| campaign2-v94-2000000 | 458292 | 17,500 | ~12,794 | 1,202 | ~878.8 | `2026-09-04T13:24:13Z v94 chunk 34 seed-base=2017000 count=500` |
| campaign2-v94-3000000 | 458293 | 18,000 | ~13,260 | 1,295 | ~954.0 | `2026-09-04T13:23:36Z v94 chunk 35 seed-base=3017500 count=500` |
| campaign2-v96-2000000 | 458294 | 24,500 | ~18,004 | 1,862 | ~1,368.3 | `2026-09-04T13:23:48Z v96 chunk 48 seed-base=2024000 count=500` |
| campaign2-v96-3000000 | 458295 | 25,000 | ~18,267 | 1,816 | ~1,326.9 | `2026-09-04T13:24:16Z v96 chunk 49 seed-base=3024500 count=500` |
| campaign2-v99-2000000 | 458296 | 18,500 | ~13,581 | 1,616 | ~1,186.3 | `2026-09-04T13:23:53Z v99 chunk 36 seed-base=2018000 count=500` |
| campaign2-v99-3000000 | 458297 | 17,500 | ~13,255 | 1,101 | ~833.9 | `2026-09-04T13:21:22Z v99 chunk 34 seed-base=3017000 count=500` |

Notes:
- "seeds tried" = `(last chunk index + 1) * 500`; "seeds/hour" and
  "finds/hour" are computed from each runner's first-vs-last log timestamp
  (all 8 started at `2026-09-04T12:02:09Z`) and current find count
  (`ls finds | wc -l`), so they are averages over the run so far, not an
  instantaneous rate.
- v84 is markedly slower than v94/v96/v99 (2 orders of magnitude fewer
  seeds/hour) — consistent with v84 (Hermes bytecode format for very old
  RN, pre-dating the v94+ instruction set) being the newest addition to the
  matrix and likely hitting slower/less-optimised code paths in the
  construct-fuzz driver or hermesc toolchain for that version; not
  investigated further here (out of scope for a signature-diff report).
- v96 has by far the highest divergent rate (82.9% pass rate vs 96–99% for
  the others, per the per-version table below) and correspondingly the
  highest finds/hour — consistent with v96 already being flagged as the
  worst-behaved version in prior triage.
